Frame-Relative Forms in the Ring Model ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- Frame coordinates: frame A uses (x, y) = (b−a, c−a); frame B uses (u, v) = (a−b, c−b); frame C uses (p, q) = (a−c, b−c). Invariant operator | Frame A form | Frame B form | Frame C form ------------------------+---------------------+---------------------+-------------------- P_{AB}(r): b − a = r | x = r | u = −r | q − p = r P_{AC}(r): c − a = r | y = r | v − u = r | p = −r P_{BC}(r): c − b = r | y − x = r | v = r | q = −r S_A (shift A by +1) | (x, y) → (x−1, y−1) | u → u+1 | p → p+1 S_B (shift B by +1) | x → x+1 | (u, v) → (u−1, v−1) | q → q+1 S_C (shift C by +1) | y → y+1 | v → v+1 | (p, q) → (p−1, q−1) Table A.1. Frame-relative forms of the invariant operators used in the model. Each row is a single invariant operator; the three entries are its conjugates under the frame changes of §6.2. Frame changes: V_{A→B}|x, y⟩ = |−x, y−x⟩; V_{B→C}|u, v⟩ = |u−v, −v⟩; V_{A→C}|x, y⟩ = |−y, x−y⟩; and V_{B→A} = V_{A→B}^{−1} maps |u, v⟩ to |−u, v−u⟩. ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- Appendix B. Verification Script and Output ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- The following Python script builds the model of Part III in the kinematical space, constructs the reduction maps for all three frames, and performs the checks of Table 1. Each frame reduction is represented by its inverse embedding J_F = R_F^{−1}, so that R_F = J_F† on H_phys. Items 1–8 concern the discrete-stage model, item 9 the continuous-time model of §9, item 10 generic-parameter non- injectivity (§8.3), item 11 strong non-supervenience (Proposition 4.12), item 12 the two constructions of Proposition 11.4 for the history-dependent law of Example 11.3, item 13 the forward representation of future conditioning (Proposition 11.1), item 14 realized-history consistency for causally local instruments (Theorem 13.4) and consistency of the laws on nested order ideals (Proposition 13.7), item 15 the equal-ensemble, different-history example (Proposition 13.2), item 16 the imperfect clocks of Proposition 14.2, with the clock probabilities computed exactly from the 32-site propagator and compared with the infinite-lattice Bessel expressions, item 17 the three- region test of Theorem 13.9 and Example 13.10, and item 18 the same test with a Gaussian field collapse at C (Proposition 13.12, Corollary 13.14). Reproducibility. The script requires Python 3 with NumPy and SciPy; the output below was produced with Python 3.13.16, NumPy 2.5.3, and SciPy 1.18.1. Random numbers come from NumPy’s default generator, with seed 7 for the initial state and for the present state used in item 11, seeds 1000–1199 for the 200 trajectories of item 9, and seed 2026 for the random instruments, initial state, and density operator of items 14 and 15. Parameters: N = 5, λ = 0.6, s = 0.8 (s = 0 and J = 0 in items 6 and 12b), pair weights (1/2, 1/4, 1/4) for the second instrument of item 12, J = 0.7, g = 0.9, τ = 0.5, Λ = 1.2, T = 5, with ħ = 1. Item 14 uses six qubits and eight random two-outcome instruments arranged as a three-layer brickwork of nearest-neighbour slots; item 16 uses a ring of 32 clock sites with θ = 0.9; item 17 uses two oscillator modes in the vacuum, φ(f_A) = q_1, φ(f_C) = p_1 + q_2, φ(f_B) = p_2, with λ_A = 0.9, λ_C = 0.8, λ_B = 0.7, kick α = 1.5, and Fock cutoffs of 40 and 50 quanta per mode; item 18 uses the same realization with σ = 0.6, hit probabilities 1 and 0.4, cutoff 40, and the outcome integral evaluated on a grid of step 0.02 over [−10, 10]. Particles are indexed 0, 1, 2 for A, B, C, and an event (i, j, d) in the output is a flash on the pair (i, j) centred at separation d. Analytical results are proved in the text; the script checks them numerically to double-precision tolerance. # Numerical checks for the Z_N three-particle QRF model with # flash-type stochastic collapse (companion to "Quantum Reference Frames # and the Lucretian Truth Profile in CTGP"). import itertools import numpy as np from scipy.linalg import expm N = 5 # sites on the ring Z_N lam = 0.6 # hit probability per stage s = 0.8 # flash width (in lattice units) Jhop, g, tau = 0.7, 0.9, 0.5 rng = np.random.default_rng(7) def idx(a, b, c): return ((a % N) * N + (b % N)) * N + (c % N) def ridx(x, y): return (x % N) * N + (y % N) D, d2 = N**3, N**2 # global translation U_g and projector onto the invariant (physical) sector def U(gg): M = np.zeros((D, D)) for a, b, c in itertools.product(range(N), repeat=3): M[idx(a+gg, b+gg, c+gg), idx(a, b, c)] = 1 return M Pi = sum(U(gg) for gg in range(N)) / N # embeddings J_F : reduced space of frame F -> physical sector (J_F = R_F^{-1}) def J(frame): M = np.zeros((D, d2)) for f in range(N): for x, y in itertools.product(range(N), repeat=2): if frame == 'A': k = idx(f, f+x, f+y) # (x_B, x_C) rel. to A if frame == 'B': k = idx(f+x, f, f+y) # (x_A, x_C) rel. to B if frame == 'C': k = idx(f+x, f+y, f) # (x_A, x_B) rel. to C M[k, ridx(x, y)] += 1/np.sqrt(N) return M JA, JB, JC = J('A'), J('B'), J('C') for M in (JA, JB, JC): assert np.allclose(M.T @ M, np.eye(d2)) and np.allclose(M @ M.T, Pi) V_AB, V_BC, V_AC = JB.T @ JA, JC.T @ JB, JC.T @ JA perm = np.zeros((d2, d2)) for x, y in itertools.product(range(N), repeat=2): perm[ridx(-x, y-x), ridx(x, y)] = 1 print("1. reductions unitary; V_AB = explicit permutation:", np.allclose(V_AB, perm)) print(" groupoid law V_BC V_AB = V_AC:", np.allclose(V_BC @ V_AB, V_AC)) # invariant generators: hopping + relational potential, and relational flashes def shift(i): M = np.zeros((D, D)) for a, b, c in itertools.product(range(N), repeat=3): t = [a, b, c]; t2 = list(t); t2[i] += 1 M[idx(*t2), idx(*t)] = 1 return M def P(i, j, r): # projector onto x_j - x_i = r (mod N) M = np.zeros((D, D)) for t in itertools.product(range(N), repeat=3): if (t[j]-t[i]) % N == r % N: M[idx(*t), idx(*t)] = 1 return M pairs = [(0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 2)] # (A,B), (A,C), (B,C) H = sum(-Jhop*(shift(i)+shift(i).T) for i in range(3)) \ + sum(g*np.cos(2*np.pi*r/N)*P(i, j, r) for (i, j) in pairs for r in range(N)) W = expm(-1j*tau*H) def wfun(r, width): dist = np.minimum(r % N, (-r) % N) return np.exp(-dist**2/(2*width**2)) if width > 0 else (dist == 0)*1.0 def kraus(width, lam_=lam): ws = np.array([wfun(r, width) for r in range(N)]); ws = ws/ws.sum() F = {'none': np.sqrt(1-lam_)*np.eye(D)} for (i, j) in pairs: for dd in range(N): F[(i, j, dd)] = np.sqrt(lam_/3)*sum(np.sqrt(ws[(r-dd) % N])*P(i, j, r) for r in range(N)) return F F = kraus(s) comp = sum(K.conj().T @ K for K in F.values()) inv = all(np.allclose(U(1) @ K, K @ U(1)) for K in list(F.values()) + [W]) print("2. instrument complete:", np.allclose(comp, np.eye(D)), "| all generators G-invariant:", inv) red = lambda O, JF: JF.T @ O @ JF WA, WB = red(W, JA), red(W, JB) FA = {k: red(K, JA) for k, K in F.items()}; FB = {k: red(K, JB) for k, K in F.items()} ok = np.allclose(V_AB @ WA @ V_AB.T, WB) and \ all(np.allclose(V_AB @ FA[k] @ V_AB.T, FB[k]) for k in F) print("3. intertwining V K^A V^dag = K^B for W and every Kraus operator:", ok) # exhaustive record distribution over n stages, frame A vs frame B vs neutral def record_dist(psi0, Wm, Fm, n): out = {} def rec(psi, hist, p): if len(hist) == n: out[hist] = (p, psi); return phi = Wm @ psi for k, K in Fm.items(): v = K @ phi; q = np.vdot(v, v).real if q > 1e-14: rec(v/np.sqrt(q), hist+(k,), p*q) rec(psi0, (), 1.0); return out phiA0 = rng.normal(size=d2) + 1j*rng.normal(size=d2); phiA0 /= np.linalg.norm(phiA0) n = 3 dA = record_dist(phiA0, WA, FA, n) dB = record_dist(V_AB @ phiA0, WB, FB, n) dN = record_dist(JA @ phiA0, W, F, n) maxdp = max(abs(dA[h][0]-dB[h][0]) for h in dA) maxdpN = max(abs(dA[h][0]-dN[h][0]) for h in dA) maxdev = max(1-abs(np.vdot(V_AB @ dA[h][1], dB[h][1])) for h in dA) print(f"4. {len(dA)} histories of {n} stages; max |p_A-p_B| = {maxdp:.2e}, " f"max |p_A-p_neutral| = {maxdpN:.2e}") print(f" max(1-|<V phi_A|phi_B>|) = {maxdev:.2e}, " f"total prob = {sum(v[0] for v in dA.values()):.12f}") # naive 'same-form' rule in frame B (B copies A's matrices in its own coordinates) dBnaive = record_dist(V_AB @ phiA0, WA, FA, n) tv = 0.5*sum(abs(dB.get(h, (0,))[0]-dBnaive.get(h, (0,))[0]) for h in set(dB) | set(dBnaive)) print(f"5. naive same-form frame-B rule: total-variation distance " f"from correct record measure = {tv:.3f}") # non-injectivity: same present state, different histories (sharp flashes, no hopping) Hd = sum(g*np.cos(2*np.pi*r/N)*P(i, j, r) for (i, j) in pairs for r in range(N)) Wd = red(expm(-1j*tau*Hd), JA) Fs = {k: red(K, JA) for k, K in kraus(0).items()} def run(psi, hist): for k in hist: psi = Fs[k] @ (Wd @ psi); psi /= np.linalg.norm(psi) return psi h1, h2 = [(0, 1, 1), (0, 2, 3)], [(0, 2, 3), (0, 1, 1)] s1, s2 = run(phiA0, h1), run(phiA0, h2) print("6. records", h1, "vs", h2, "-> same present ray:", np.isclose(abs(np.vdot(s1, s2)), 1.0)) # frame-relative product state becomes entangled in the other frame def ket(x, y): v = np.zeros(d2); v[ridx(x, y)] = 1; return v prodA = (ket(1, 0) + ket(2, 0))/np.sqrt(2) def ent(v): sv = np.linalg.svd(v.reshape(N, N), compute_uv=False)**2; sv = sv[sv > 1e-12] return -(sv*np.log2(sv)).sum() print(f"7. entanglement entropy: frame A {ent(prodA):.3f} bits, " f"frame B {ent(V_AB @ prodA):.3f} bits") # an absolute (non-invariant) collapse operator leaves the physical sector Pabs = np.zeros((D, D)) for t in itertools.product(range(N), repeat=3): if t[2] == 0: Pabs[idx(*t), idx(*t)] = 1 v = Pabs @ (JA @ phiA0) leak = np.linalg.norm(v - Pi @ v)/np.linalg.norm(v) print(f"8. absolute collapse: ||(1-Pi) P_abs Psi|| / ||P_abs Psi|| = {leak:.3f}") # ---- 9. continuous-time relational jump process (GRW-type), frames A and B Lam, T = 1.2, 5.0 JA_ops = {k: red(K, JA) for k, K in kraus(s, lam_=1.0).items() if k != 'none'} JB_ops = {k: V_AB @ K @ V_AB.T for k, K in JA_ops.items()} # = frame-B forms HA, HB = red(H, JA), red(H, JB) assert np.allclose(sum(K.conj().T @ K for K in JA_ops.values()), np.eye(d2)) def trajectory(psi, Hm, ops, r): t, events = 0.0, [] while True: dt = r.exponential(1/Lam) # hit times: Poisson, rate Lam if t + dt > T: psi = expm(-1j*Hm*(T-t)) @ psi; break t += dt; psi = expm(-1j*Hm*dt) @ psi keys = list(ops) p = np.array([np.linalg.norm(ops[k] @ psi)**2 for k in keys]) k = keys[r.choice(len(keys), p=p/p.sum())] psi = ops[k] @ psi; psi /= np.linalg.norm(psi) events.append((round(t, 12), k)) return psi, events same, worst, nev = True, 0.0, 0 for trial in range(200): pa, ea = trajectory(phiA0, HA, JA_ops, np.random.default_rng(1000+trial)) pb, eb = trajectory(V_AB @ phiA0, HB, JB_ops, np.random.default_rng(1000+trial)) same &= (ea == eb); nev += len(ea) worst = max(worst, 1-abs(np.vdot(V_AB @ pa, pb))) print(f"9. continuous time: 200 trajectories, {nev} events; " f"identical event records in A and B: {same}") print(f" max(1-|<V psi_A|psi_B>|) at final time = {worst:.1e}") # ---- 10. generic parameters: do distinct histories reach the same present ray? fin = [(h, v[1]) for h, v in dA.items()] G = np.array([v for _, v in fin]); ov = np.abs(G.conj() @ G.T) np.fill_diagonal(ov, 0); best = ov.max() ncoinc = int((ov > 1 - 1e-10).sum() // 2) F2 = best**2; helstrom = 0.5*(1 - np.sqrt(1 - F2)) print(f"10. generic parameters, {len(fin)} three-stage histories: " f"exact coincidences = {ncoinc}; largest overlap = {best:.4f}") print(f" minimum error probability for telling that pair apart (Helstrom) = {helstrom:.3f}") # ---- 11. finite-width flashes: every present state is reachable by every history Phi = rng.normal(size=d2) + 1j*rng.normal(size=d2); Phi /= np.linalg.norm(Phi) hists = [((0, 1, 1), (1, 2, 4), 'none'), ('none', (0, 2, 0), (0, 1, 3))] res = [] for h in hists: psi = Phi.copy() for k in reversed(h): # back-propagate: psi_prev ~ W^-1 F_k^-1 psi psi = WA.conj().T @ np.linalg.solve(FA[k], psi); psi /= np.linalg.norm(psi) psi0, pr = psi.copy(), 1.0 for k in h: # forward run from the reconstructed start v = FA[k] @ (WA @ psi); q = np.vdot(v, v).real; pr *= q; psi = v/np.sqrt(q) res.append((abs(np.vdot(psi, Phi)), pr, psi0)) print(f"11. two different 3-stage histories, same present ray: " f"|<end|Phi>| = {res[0][0]:.12f}, {res[1][0]:.12f}") print(f" history probabilities {res[0][1]:.2e}, {res[1][1]:.2e}; " f"overlap of the two initial states = {abs(np.vdot(res[0][2], res[1][2])):.3f}") # ---- 12. a history-dependent (QRF-covariant) law is not a kernel on the present state # second instrument: same flashes, pair weights q = (1/2, 1/4, 1/4) instead of 1/3 each def kraus_q(width, q): ws = np.array([wfun(r, width) for r in range(N)]); ws = ws/ws.sum() out = {'none': np.sqrt(1-lam)*np.eye(D)} for (i, j), qq in zip(pairs, q): for dd in range(N): out[(i, j, dd)] = np.sqrt(lam*qq)*sum(np.sqrt(ws[(r-dd) % N])*P(i, j, r) for r in range(N)) return out def law(Fa, Fb): # instrument for transitions after the first return lambda first: Fa if (first != 'none' and first[:2] == (0, 1)) else Fb def run_law(psi, hist, Fa, Fb, Wm): pr = 1.0 for m, k in enumerate(hist): Fm = Fa if m == 0 else law(Fa, Fb)(hist[0]) v = Fm[k] @ (Wm @ psi); q = np.vdot(v, v).real; pr *= q; psi = v/np.sqrt(q) return psi, pr def next_law(psi, first, Fa, Fb, Wm): Fm = law(Fa, Fb)(first) return np.array([np.linalg.norm(Fm[k] @ (Wm @ psi))**2 for k in Fa]) # (a) finite width, across initial conditions: invert the two branch products FA_q = {k: red(K, JA) for k, K in kraus_q(s, (0.5, 0.25, 0.25)).items()} Phi_c = WA.conj().T @ ket(1, 3) # present ray; W Phi_c = |1,3> br1, br2 = ((0, 1, 2), (1, 2, 0)), ('none', (1, 2, 0)) starts = [] for hb in br1, br2: Fsecond = law(FA, FA_q)(hb[0]) M = Fsecond[hb[1]] @ WA @ FA[hb[0]] @ WA psi0 = np.linalg.solve(M, Phi_c); psi0 /= np.linalg.norm(psi0) end, pr = run_law(psi0, hb, FA, FA_q, WA); starts.append((abs(np.vdot(end, Phi_c)), pr)) tv_a = 0.5*np.abs(next_law(Phi_c, br1[0], FA, FA_q, WA) - next_law(Phi_c, br2[0], FA, FA_q, WA)).sum() print(f"12a. finite width: both branches end at the same ray " f"(|<end|Phi>| = {starts[0][0]:.12f}, {starts[1][0]:.12f}),") print(f" probabilities {starts[0][1]:.2e}, {starts[1][1]:.2e}; " f"next-event total variation = {tv_a:.4f} (lambda/6 = {lam/6:.4f})") # (b) sharp flashes, J = 0, one fixed initial state Fs1 = {k: red(K, JA) for k, K in kraus(0).items()} Fs2 = {k: red(K, JA) for k, K in kraus_q(0, (0.5, 0.25, 0.25)).items()} w1, w2 = ((0, 1, 1), (0, 2, 3)), ((0, 2, 3), (0, 1, 1)) e1, p1 = run_law(phiA0, w1, Fs1, Fs2, Wd); e2, p2 = run_law(phiA0, w2, Fs1, Fs2, Wd) tv_b = 0.5*np.abs(next_law(e1, w1[0], Fs1, Fs2, Wd) - next_law(e2, w2[0], Fs1, Fs2, Wd)).sum() print(f"12b. sharp, fixed initial state: same present ray: " f"{np.isclose(abs(np.vdot(e1, e2)), 1.0)}; probabilities {p1:.3e}, {p2:.3e};") print(f" next-event total variation = {tv_b:.4f}") # ---- 13. conditioning on a future event = forward Markov law with a Doob h-transform E, nT = (1, 2, 0), 3 def h(psi, m): # Pr(event E at stage nT | psi at m) if m == nT - 1: return np.linalg.norm(FA[E] @ (WA @ psi))**2 tot = 0.0 for k in F: v = FA[k] @ (WA @ psi); q = np.vdot(v, v).real if q > 1e-15: tot += q*h(v/np.sqrt(q), m+1) return tot cond = {hh: v[0] for hh, v in dA.items() if hh[-1] == E} Z = sum(cond.values()); cond = {hh: p_/Z for hh, p_ in cond.items()} def doob(hist): psi, p_ = phiA0.copy(), 1.0 for m, k in enumerate(hist): v = FA[k] @ (WA @ psi); q = np.vdot(v, v).real; nxt = v/np.sqrt(q) hn = (1.0 if k == E else 0.0) if m == nT-1 else h(nxt, m+1) p_ *= q*hn/h(psi, m); psi = nxt return p_ dmax = max(abs(doob(hh) - pc) for hh, pc in cond.items()) print(f"13. conditioned on {E} at stage {nT}: Doob forward kernel vs conditioned law, " f"max diff = {dmax:.1e}") # ---- 14. realized-history consistency for causally local instruments (brickwork circuit) rq = np.random.default_rng(2026) nq = 6 slots = [(1, 0), (1, 2), (1, 4), (2, 1), (2, 3), (3, 0), (3, 2), (3, 4)] # (layer, first qubit) def qubits(x): return {slots[x][1], slots[x][1] + 1} direct = {(x, y) for x in range(len(slots)) for y in range(len(slots)) if slots[x][0] < slots[y][0] and qubits(x) & qubits(y)} prec = set(direct) while True: # transitive closure new = {(a, d) for (a, b) in prec for (c, d) in prec if b == c} - prec if not new: break prec |= new def rand_instr(r): # random 2-outcome instrument, 2 qubits Z_ = r.normal(size=(8, 4)) + 1j*r.normal(size=(8, 4)); Q, _ = np.linalg.qr(Z_) return [Q[0:4, :], Q[4:8, :]] def embed(K, q0, n=nq): return np.kron(np.kron(np.eye(2**q0), K), np.eye(2**(n - q0 - 2))) instr = {x: rand_instr(rq) for x in range(len(slots))} psi_c = rq.normal(size=2**nq) + 1j*rq.normal(size=2**nq); psi_c /= np.linalg.norm(psi_c) def lin_ext(r): left, order = set(range(len(slots))), [] while left: ready = [x for x in left if not any((y, x) in prec for y in left)] x = ready[r.integers(len(ready))]; order.append(x); left.remove(x) return tuple(order) def joint(order, ins, where=None): where = where or {x: slots[x][1] for x in range(len(slots))} out = {} for outs in itertools.product((0, 1), repeat=len(slots)): v = psi_c.copy() for x in order: v = embed(ins[x][outs[x]], where[x]) @ v out[outs] = v return out def all_ext(left, prefix): if not left: yield tuple(prefix); return for x in sorted(left): if not any((y, x) in prec for y in left): yield from all_ext(left - {x}, prefix + [x]) exts = set(all_ext(set(range(len(slots))), [])) ref = joint(next(iter(exts)), instr) dp, ds = 0.0, 0.0 for e in exts: jj = joint(e, instr) for o in ref: pa, pb = np.vdot(ref[o], ref[o]).real, np.vdot(jj[o], jj[o]).real dp = max(dp, abs(pa - pb)) if pa > 1e-12: ds = max(ds, 1 - abs(np.vdot(ref[o], jj[o]))/np.sqrt(pa*pb)) print(f"14. causally local instruments: all {len(exts)} foliations (linear extensions), " f"{len(ref)} outcome histories;") print(f" max |p - p'| = {dp:.1e}, max state deviation = {ds:.1e}") # no-signaling: marginal at slot 3 is unchanged when instruments outside its causal past change X = 3; past = {y for y in range(len(slots)) if (y, X) in prec} | {X} def marg(ins): jj = joint(next(iter(exts)), ins); m = np.zeros(2) for o, v in jj.items(): m[o[X]] += np.vdot(v, v).real return m m0 = marg(instr); worst = 0.0 for y in set(range(len(slots))) - past: ins2 = dict(instr); ins2[y] = rand_instr(rq); worst = max(worst, np.abs(marg(ins2) - m0).max()) print(f" instruments changed outside the predecessor set of slot {X}: " f"max change = {worst:.1e}") # consistency on nested order ideals: law on I computed from instruments in I alone I_ideal = sorted(past) devI = 0.0 full = joint(next(iter(exts)), instr) for oI in itertools.product((0, 1), repeat=len(I_ideal)): v = psi_c.copy() for x, ax in zip(I_ideal, oI): v = embed(instr[x][ax], slots[x][1]) @ v pI = np.vdot(v, v).real pm = sum(np.vdot(w, w).real for o, w in full.items() if tuple(o[x] for x in I_ideal) == oI) devI = max(devI, abs(pI - pm)) print(f" order ideal {I_ideal}: law from its own instruments vs marginal of full law, " f"max diff = {devI:.1e}") # counterexample: two 'spacelike' slots acting on overlapping qubits where_bad = {x: slots[x][1] for x in range(len(slots))}; where_bad[1] = 1 # slot 1 on (1,2) o1 = (0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7); o2 = (1, 0, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7) j1, j2 = joint(o1, instr, where_bad), joint(o2, instr, where_bad) tvb = 0.5*sum(abs(np.vdot(j1[o], j1[o]).real - np.vdot(j2[o], j2[o]).real) for o in j1) print(f" non-commuting 'spacelike' pair: total variation between orders = {tvb:.3f}") # ---- 15. equal ensembles, different realized-history measures (two unravellings) plus = np.array([1, 1])/np.sqrt(2); minus = np.array([1, -1])/np.sqrt(2) I1 = [np.diag([1, 0]), np.diag([0, 1])] I2 = [np.eye(2)/np.sqrt(2), np.diag([1, -1])/np.sqrt(2)] Xm = [np.outer(plus, plus), np.outer(minus, minus)] rho = rq.normal(size=(2, 2)) + 1j*rq.normal(size=(2, 2)) rho = rho @ rho.conj().T; rho /= np.trace(rho) ch = lambda I, r: sum(K @ r @ K.conj().T for K in I) same_channel = np.allclose(ch(I1, rho), ch(I2, rho)) def jd(I): return np.array([[np.linalg.norm(M @ K @ plus)**2 for M in Xm] for K in I]) J1, J2 = jd(I1), jd(I2) def mi(Jm): pa, pb = Jm.sum(1), Jm.sum(0) return sum(Jm[i, j]*np.log2(Jm[i, j]/(pa[i]*pb[j])) for i in range(2) for j in range(2) if Jm[i, j] > 1e-15) print(f"15. same channel: {same_channel}; joint law of (first event, later X outcome):") print(f" {J1.round(3).tolist()} vs {J2.round(3).tolist()};") print(f" mutual information {mi(J1):.3f} vs {mi(J2):.3f} bits") # ---- 16. imperfect quantum clocks: identity of events vs clock-relative timing Mc, th = 32, 0.9 Sc = np.roll(np.eye(Mc), 1, axis=0) Uc = Sc @ expm(-1j*th*(Sc + Sc.T)) step = np.abs(Uc[:, 0])**2 # law of one recorded clock step signed = [(m if m <= Mc//2 else m - Mc) for m in range(Mc)] p_inv = sum(step[m] for m in range(Mc) if signed[m] < 0) p_stall = step[0] from scipy.special import jv bes_inv = (1 - jv(0, 2*th)**2 - 2*jv(1, 2*th)**2)/2; bes_stall = jv(1, 2*th)**2 far = sum(step[m] for m in range(Mc) if abs(signed[m]) >= 8) print(f"16. drifting quantum clock, exact on the 32-site ring (signed displacement in -15..16):") print(f" P(backward) = {p_inv:.9f}, P(no advance) = {p_stall:.9f};") print(f" infinite-lattice Bessel values {bes_inv:.9f}, {bes_stall:.9f}") print(f" max difference = {max(abs(p_inv-bes_inv), abs(p_stall-bes_stall)):.1e}; " f"P(|displacement| >= 8) = {far:.1e}") per = [n % 2 for n in range(5)] print(f" period-2 counter readings at stages 0-4: {per};") print(" superposed counter: reading at stage n is n or n+4, each with probability 0.5") # ---- 17. gapless detectors on a free field: three-region (Sorkin-type) signaling test # two-mode realization of the commutation pattern E_AB = 0, E_AC = E_CB = 1: # phi_A = q1, phi_C = p1 + q2, phi_B = p2; vacuum state; Fock cutoff nc per mode def sorkin(nc, lamA=0.9, lamC=0.8, lamB=0.7, kick=1.5): am = np.diag(np.sqrt(np.arange(1, nc)), 1); I_ = np.eye(nc) qm = (am + am.T)/np.sqrt(2); pm = 1j*(am.T - am)/np.sqrt(2) phA, phC, phB = np.kron(qm, I_), np.kron(pm, I_) + np.kron(I_, qm), np.kron(I_, pm) v0 = np.zeros(nc*nc, complex); v0[0] = 1 def fn(Hm, f): w, V = np.linalg.eigh(Hm); return (V * f(w)) @ V.conj().T det = lambda ph, l: [fn(ph, lambda x: np.cos(l*x)), 1j*fn(ph, lambda x: np.sin(l*x))] gg = lambda x: 0.5*(1 + np.tanh(2*x)) lud = lambda ph: [fn(ph, lambda x: np.sqrt(gg(x))), fn(ph, lambda x: np.sqrt(1 - gg(x)))] def bob(KA, KC, KB): pb = 0.0 for Ka in KA: va = Ka @ v0 for Kc in KC: pb += np.linalg.norm(KB[1] @ (Kc @ va))**2 return pb KB = det(phB, lamB); Id = [np.eye(nc*nc)]; kA = [fn(phA, lambda x: np.exp(1j*kick*x))] out = {} for nm, KC in (('gapless', det(phC, lamC)), ('luders', lud(phC))): base = bob(Id, KC, KB) out[nm] = (base, bob(det(phA, lamA), KC, KB) - base, bob(kA, KC, KB) - base) return out s40, s50 = sorkin(40), sorkin(50) ana = 0.5 - 0.5*np.cos(2*0.7*0.8)*np.exp(-0.7**2) print(f"17. three-region test, Bob's P(b=1) and its shift under Alice's detector / kick:") print(f" gapless Charlie: P = {s50['gapless'][0]:.10f} (analytic {ana:.10f}); shifts " f"{s50['gapless'][1]:.1e}, {s50['gapless'][2]:.1e}") print(f" unsharp Lueders Charlie: P = {s50['luders'][0]:.6f}; shifts " f"{s50['luders'][1]:.6f}, {s50['luders'][2]:.6f}") print(f" cutoff 40 vs 50: max change = " f"{max(abs(s40[k][i] - s50[k][i]) for k in s40 for i in range(3)):.1e}") # ---- 18. Gaussian collapse of a smeared field at C (Weyl-diagonal): same three-region test def gauss_test(nc=40, lamA=0.9, lamB=0.7, kick=1.5, sig=0.6, p_hit=1.0, dy=0.02): am = np.diag(np.sqrt(np.arange(1, nc)), 1); I_ = np.eye(nc) qm = (am + am.T)/np.sqrt(2); pm = 1j*(am.T - am)/np.sqrt(2) phA, phC, phB = np.kron(qm, I_), np.kron(pm, I_) + np.kron(I_, qm), np.kron(I_, pm) v0 = np.zeros(nc*nc, complex); v0[0] = 1 def fn(Hm, f): w, V = np.linalg.eigh(Hm); return (V * f(w)) @ V.conj().T wC, VC = np.linalg.eigh(phC); ys = np.arange(-10, 10 + dy/2, dy) KB1 = 1j*fn(phB, lambda x: np.sin(lamB*x)) def bob(KA): pb = 0.0 for Ka in KA: w = VC.conj().T @ (Ka @ v0) if p_hit < 1: pb += (1 - p_hit)*np.linalg.norm(KB1 @ (VC @ w))**2 for y in ys: k = (2*np.pi*sig**2)**-0.25*np.exp(-(wC - y)**2/(4*sig**2)) pb += p_hit*dy*np.linalg.norm(KB1 @ (VC @ (k*w)))**2 return pb detA = [fn(phA, lambda x: np.cos(lamA*x)), 1j*fn(phA, lambda x: np.sin(lamA*x))] base = bob([np.eye(nc*nc)]) ana = 0.5 - 0.5*np.exp(-lamB**2)*((1 - p_hit) + p_hit*np.exp(-lamB**2/(2*sig**2))) shifts = (bob(detA) - base, bob([fn(phA, lambda x: np.exp(1j*kick*x))]) - base) return base, ana, shifts for ph in (1.0, 0.4): b_, a_, sh = gauss_test(p_hit=ph) print(f"18. Gaussian collapse at C (hit probability {ph}): P = {b_:.10f} " f"(analytic {a_:.10f});") print(f" shifts under Alice's detector / kick: {sh[0]:.1e}, {sh[1]:.1e}") import sys, scipy print(f"Environment: Python {sys.version.split()[0]}, NumPy {np.__version__}, " f"SciPy {scipy.__version__}") print("Seeds: 7 (initial state; present state of item 11); 1000-1199 (item 9); " "2026 (items 14-15)") Output: 1. reductions unitary; V_AB = explicit permutation: True groupoid law V_BC V_AB = V_AC: True 2. instrument complete: True | all generators G-invariant: True 3. intertwining V K^A V^dag = K^B for W and every Kraus operator: True 4. 4096 histories of 3 stages; max |p_A-p_B| = 2.78e-17, max |p_A-p_neutral| = 1.39e-17 max(1-|<V phi_A|phi_B>|) = 6.66e-16, total prob = 1.000000000000 5. naive same-form frame-B rule: total-variation distance from correct record measure = 0.180 6. records [(0, 1, 1), (0, 2, 3)] vs [(0, 2, 3), (0, 1, 1)] -> same present ray: True 7. entanglement entropy: frame A 0.000 bits, frame B 1.000 bits 8. absolute collapse: ||(1-Pi) P_abs Psi|| / ||P_abs Psi|| = 0.894 9. continuous time: 200 trajectories, 1158 events; identical event records in A and B: True max(1-|<V psi_A|psi_B>|) at final time = 6.7e-16 10. generic parameters, 4096 three-stage histories: exact coincidences = 0; largest overlap = 0.9852 minimum error probability for telling that pair apart (Helstrom) = 0.414 11. two different 3-stage histories, same present ray: |<end|Phi>| = 1.000000000000, 1.000000000000 history probabilities 4.15e-05, 7.62e-05; overlap of the two initial states = 0.552 12a. finite width: both branches end at the same ray (|<end|Phi>| = 1.000000000000, 1.000000000000), probabilities 5.04e-05, 1.62e-03; next-event total variation = 0.1000 (lambda/6 = 0.1000) 12b. sharp, fixed initial state: same present ray: True; probabilities 8.254e-04, 1.238e-03; next-event total variation = 0.1000 13. conditioned on (1, 2, 0) at stage 3: Doob forward kernel vs conditioned law, max diff = 8.3e-17 14. causally local instruments: all 136 foliations (linear extensions), 256 outcome histories; max |p - p'| = 6.9e-18, max state deviation = 4.4e-16 instruments changed outside the predecessor set of slot 3: max change = 7.8e-16 order ideal [0, 1, 3]: law from its own instruments vs marginal of full law, max diff = 1.1e-16 non-commuting 'spacelike' pair: total variation between orders = 0.165 15. same channel: True; infinite-lattice Bessel values 0.104042655, 0.338161965 max difference = 1.1e-16; P(|displacement| >= 8) = 7.4e-09 period-2 counter readings at stages 0-4: [0, 1, 0, 1, 0]; superposed counter: reading at stage n is n or n+4, each with probability 0.5 17. three-region test, Bob's P(b=1) and its shift under Alice's detector / kick: gapless Charlie: P = 0.3665447170 (analytic 0.3665447170); Gaussian collapse at C (hit probability 0.4): P = 0.2541731691 (analytic 0.2541731691); shifts under Alice's detector / kick: -8.3e-16, -2.0e-14 Environment: Python 3.13.16, NumPy 2.5.3, SciPy 1.18.1 Seeds: 7 (initial state; present state of item 11); 1000-1199 (item 9); 2026 (items 14-15) ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- Appendix C. Verification of the Collapse and Two-Clock Results ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- The results of §§13.8–13.11 and §14.5 are checked by two separate scripts, one for each branch of the program, so that each can be rerun and audited independently of Appendix B. As there, the checks confirm the analytical results in specific instances and guard against sign and ordering errors. They do not prove anything about all states, all configurations, or the continuum. Table C.1 lists, for each check, the quantity computed, the identity tested, the result, and the analytical argument that establishes the identity. 0.5 | Prop. 14.5(e) | Re⟨W_1ψ, W_2ψ⟩)/2 in all three descriptions; | | | incoherent mixture of offsets | | Table C.1. Checks of §§13.8–13.11 (script C.1) and §14.5 (script C.2), with the result each instantiates. Two analytical ingredients of the scripts should be stated. First, for a quasi-free state with vanishing one-point function, the characteristic function of the outcomes of Gaussian collapses applied in a given order follows from the identity ∫ e^{isy} K_y W(g) K_y dy = exp(−E(g, h)²/(8σ²) − s²σ²/2) W(g + sh), applied from the last collapse to the first. The outcomes are then jointly Gaussian with covariance C = Σ + diag(σ_m²) + Σ_m e_m e_mᵀ / (4σ_m²), where Σ is the covariance of the smeared fields in the state, and (e_m)_{m′} = E(h_{m′}, h_m) if collapse m′ follows collapse m in the order and 0 otherwise. When the order is a linear extension of an admissible configuration, every nonzero entry of e_m belongs to a collapse in the causal future of collapse m, so C does not depend on the linear extension; items C3(c) and C3(d) test this formula against direct computation. Second, for a massless field in 1 + 1 dimensions the causal propagator is E(t, x) = −(sgn u + sgn v)/4 in light-cone coordinates u = t − x and v = t + x, up to an overall sign convention. For smearings that are products β(u)β(v) supported in causal diamonds, E(h_i, h_j) therefore reduces to one-dimensional integrals. It equals zero exactly when the diamonds are causally separated. Third, for the gapless pointers of Proposition 13.31, the retarded part of the propagator is E_ret(t, x) = −θ(u)θ(v)/2, so Γ_kl also reduces to products of one-dimensional integrals, and in a one-mode realization the field vector exp(−i Σ_k p_k φ(h_k))|0⟩ produced from the vacuum at given pointer momenta is a coherent state. The script uses this to compute the joint state of the field and the pointers on a grid of pointer momenta. The sums of item C4(a) place the smearings on Gauss–Legendre nodes and add E over the pairs of nodes ordered by each foliation. Reproducibility. The software is that of Appendix B: Python 3.13.16, NumPy 2.5.3, and SciPy 1.18.1. Script C.1 runs in about four minutes, most of it in item C3(h), and script C.2 in about 5 seconds. Seeds are 31 and 32 for the random states of item C2, 2027 for the configurations of item C3, and 5 for the system states of script C.2. Item C1 uses two oscillator modes with Fock cutoff 24 per mode, A = 0.6 q_1 − 0.3 q_2 + 0.2 p_1 + 0.5 p_2, σ = 0.8, an outcome grid of step 0.01, and 80-point Gauss–Hermite quadrature for the kicks. Item C2(a) uses m = 1.7, ω = 1.3, V(Q) = ½mω²Q² + 0.2 Q⁴, σ = 0.7, and cutoffs of 140 and 180 quanta. Item C2(b) uses the modes of C1 with the coupling matrix ((1.3, −0.4), (−0.4, 0.9)). Item C2(c) uses a periodic lattice of 48 sites with mass 0.6, σ = 0.9, and a product smearing of half-widths 2.5 in time and 3 sites in space. Item C2(d) uses mass 1, σ = 0.9, and a boost of rapidity 0.5. Item C3 uses causal diamonds of half-size 0.5 with smearings scaled by 4. The intensities are 6 in C3(a), 1.5 in C3(b) and C3(f), and 0.4 in C3(c), in square boxes of side 4, 1 to 2.5, 2, and 4, and the three-collapse configurations of C3(d) are drawn uniformly in a box of side 1.6. Item C3(h) uses diamonds with light-cone centres (u, v) = (0, 0), (−0.525, 1.2), (−0.225, 1.05), and (−1.05, 2.3) for j, c1, c2, and y, smearings scaled by 8, widths 0.8, 0.9, and 1 for c1, c2, and y, a kick W(3 f_j), a Gaussian collapse of width 0.8, or the unsharp Lüders instrument of Example 13.10 at j, a two-mode Fock realization in which φ(h_j) and φ(h_y) act on different modes, with cutoffs of 36 and 44 quanta per mode, and, as the negative control, the same Lüders instrument at c1. Item C4 uses the overlapping pair of C3(e) and a one-way pair with centres (t, x) = (0, 0) and (1.2, 0.3), smearings scaled by 4, pointer widths 0.8 and 0.9, 81 momenta per pointer with spacing 0.12, the one-mode realization with cutoff 140, the foliations by t and by cosh(0.6) t − sinh(0.6) x, and 32 and 64 Gauss–Legendre nodes per light-cone axis. The widths σ are drawn uniformly from [0.5, 1.2] in C3(c) and fixed at 0.8, 0.9, and 1.0 in C3(d). Items C3(d) and C3(e) use a one-mode Fock realization of the commutation data with cutoff 140 and outcome bins with edges −0.7, 0, and 0.7. Items C3(c) and C3(f) use quasi-free states with covariance ½|E| + 0.3. In script C.2 the clocks are ideal, the grid step is 0.01, and the wave packets in the offset are smooth bumps of half-width 0.25. Case 3 of script C.2 uses one system state in both offset packets. Throughout, smooth bumps are exp(−1/(1 − z²)) on (−1, 1). C.1 Collapse Networks # Verification of the collapse results of Sections 13.8-13.10 (Appendix C.1). # C1: random-kick form of the unread Gaussian collapse channel (Proposition 13.15). # C2: energy-momentum cost of Gaussian collapse (Proposition 13.16). # C3: Poisson-located Gaussian collapses on a massless field in 1+1 dimensions # (Theorem 13.21, Propositions 13.22 and 13.23, Lemma 13.26, Proposition 13.28). # C4: the same collapses realized by gapless pointers coupled to the field (Proposition 13.31). import numpy as np from scipy.linalg import eigh, schur, expm from scipy.special import erf Phi = lambda z: 0.5 * (1 + erf(z / np.sqrt(2))) # standard normal CDF bump = lambda z: np.where(np.abs(z) < 1, np.exp(-1 / np.maximum(1 - z**2, 1e-300)), 0.0) def ladder(nc): return np.diag(np.sqrt(np.arange(1, nc)), 1) def modes(nc, m): """annihilation operators of m modes, each truncated at nc quanta""" a1, I = ladder(nc), np.eye(nc) out = [] for k in range(m): A = np.array([[1.0]]) for j in range(m): A = np.kron(A, a1 if j == k else I) out.append(A) return out def dephase(rho, A, sig): """unread Gaussian collapse of A: multiplier exp(-(a - a')^2/(8 sig^2)) in A's eigenbasis""" w, V = eigh(A) M = np.exp(-(w[:, None] - w[None, :])**2 / (8 * sig**2)) return V @ ((V.conj().T @ rho @ V) * M) @ V.conj().T print('C1. Random-kick form of the unread Gaussian collapse channel') nc, sig = 24, 0.8 a1, a2 = modes(nc, 2) q = [(a + a.T) / np.sqrt(2) for a in (a1, a2)] p = [1j * (a.T - a) / np.sqrt(2) for a in (a1, a2)] A = 0.6 * q[0] - 0.3 * q[1] + 0.2 * p[0] + 0.5 * p[1] w, V = eigh(A) d = w[:, None] - w[None, :] Mex = np.exp(-d**2 / (8 * sig**2)) dy = 0.01 ys = np.arange(w.min() - 8 * sig, w.max() + 8 * sig, dy) kap = lambda y: (2 * np.pi * sig**2)**-0.25 * np.exp(-(w - y)**2 / (4 * sig**2)) Mk = sum(dy * np.outer(kap(y), kap(y)) for y in ys) tq, wq = np.polynomial.hermite.hermgauss(80) st = 1 / (2 * sig) # standard deviation of the kick parameter t Mru = sum(wt * np.exp(-1j * np.sqrt(2) * st * tt * d) for tt, wt in zip(tq, wq)) Mru = Mru / np.sqrt(np.pi) print(' Kraus integral over y vs exact multiplier: max dev %.1e' % np.abs(Mk - Mex).max()) print(' Gaussian mixture of kicks vs exact multiplier: max dev %.1e' % np.abs(Mru - Mex).max()) print('C2. Energy-momentum cost') # (a) one particle, position collapse, anharmonic potential; expected hbar^2/(8 m sig^2) mass, om, g4, sig = 1.7, 1.3, 0.2, 0.7 for nc in (140, 180): a = ladder(nc + 8) Q = (a + a.T) / np.sqrt(2 * mass * om) P = 1j * np.sqrt(mass * om / 2) * (a.T - a) H = P @ P / (2 * mass) + 0.5 * mass * om**2 * Q @ Q + g4 * np.linalg.matrix_power(Q, 4) H, Qt = H[:nc, :nc], Q[:nc, :nc] e0, U0 = eigh(H) rng = np.random.default_rng(31) c = rng.normal(size=8) + 1j * rng.normal(size=8) psi = np.zeros(nc, complex) psi[:8] = c / np.linalg.norm(c) dE = [] for vec in (U0[:, 0], psi): rho = np.outer(vec, vec.conj()) dE.append(np.trace(dephase(rho, Qt, sig) @ H).real - np.trace(rho @ H).real) print(' (a) cutoff %d: ground %.12f, random %.12f; 1/(8 m sig^2) = %.12f' % (nc, dE[0], dE[1], 1 / (8 * mass * sig**2))) # (b) two coupled modes, A linear in (q, p), H quadratic: (|a|^2 + b.K.b)/(8 sig^2) nc, sig = 24, 0.8 Kc = np.array([[1.3, -0.4], [-0.4, 0.9]]) H2 = 0.5 * (p[0] @ p[0] + p[1] @ p[1]) H2 = H2 + 0.5 * sum(Kc[i, j] * q[i] @ q[j] for i in range(2) for j in range(2)) av, bv = np.array([0.6, -0.3]), np.array([0.2, 0.5]) A = av[0] * q[0] + av[1] * q[1] + bv[0] * p[0] + bv[1] * p[1] rng = np.random.default_rng(32) psi = np.zeros(nc * nc, complex) for n1 in range(3): for n2 in range(3): psi[n1 * nc + n2] = rng.normal() + 1j * rng.normal() psi /= np.linalg.norm(psi) vac = np.zeros(nc * nc) vac[0] = 1 pred = (av @ av + bv @ Kc @ bv) / (8 * sig**2) res = [np.trace(dephase(np.outer(v, v.conj()), A, sig) @ H2).real - (v.conj() @ H2 @ v).real for v in (vac, psi)] print(' (b) two modes: vacuum %.12f, random %.12f; predicted %.12f' % (*res, pred)) # (c) lattice field in 1+1 dimensions: canonical double commutator vs Fourier formula Nl, mf, sig = 48, 0.6, 0.9 Lap = 2 * np.eye(Nl) - np.roll(np.eye(Nl), 1, 0) - np.roll(np.eye(Nl), -1, 0) Kl = mf**2 * np.eye(Nl) + Lap ev, Ev = eigh(Kl) Om = np.sqrt(ev) tn, tw = np.polynomial.legendre.leggauss(200) T0 = 2.5 ts, tw = T0 * tn, T0 * tw xs = np.arange(Nl) hx = bump((xs - 24) / 3.0) avec, bvec = np.zeros(Nl), np.zeros(Nl) for t, wt in zip(ts, tw): ht = bump(t / T0) * hx avec += wt * Ev @ (np.cos(Om * t) * (Ev.T @ ht)) bvec += wt * Ev @ (np.sin(Om * t) / Om * (Ev.T @ ht)) dE_can = (avec @ avec + bvec @ Kl @ bvec) / (8 * sig**2) ks = 2 * np.pi * np.arange(Nl) / Nl wk = np.sqrt(mf**2 + (2 * np.sin(ks / 2))**2) ht_hat = np.array([np.sum(tw * bump(ts / T0) * np.exp(1j * wk[j] * ts)) for j in range(Nl)]) hx_hat = np.array([np.sum(hx * np.exp(-1j * ks[j] * xs)) for j in range(Nl)]) dE_mode = np.sum(np.abs(ht_hat * hx_hat)**2) / (8 * sig**2 * Nl) print(' (c) lattice: canonical %.12f, Fourier formula %.12f, relative diff. %.1e' % (dE_can, dE_mode, abs(dE_can - dE_mode) / dE_can)) # (d) Delta P^mu transforms as a four-vector (continuum, 1+1 dimensions, mass 1) mf, sig = 1.0, 0.9 def hfun(t, x): return bump(t / 1.2) * bump((x - 0.3 - 0.4 * t) / 1.5) * (1 + 0.4 * x) def dP(hf, tmax, xmax, n): """(1/(4 sig^2)) int dk/(2 pi 2 omega) k^mu |h^(omega_k, k)|^2 by quadrature""" tt, xx = np.linspace(-tmax, tmax, n), np.linspace(-xmax, xmax, n) TT, XX = np.meshgrid(tt, xx, indexing='ij') kg = np.linspace(-80, 80, 8001) omk = np.sqrt(mf**2 + kg**2) Et, Ex = np.exp(1j * np.outer(omk, tt)), np.exp(-1j * np.outer(kg, xx)) hh = np.sum((Et @ hf(TT, XX)) * Ex, axis=1) * (tt[1] - tt[0]) * (xx[1] - xx[0]) wgt = np.abs(hh)**2 / (4 * np.pi * omk) * (kg[1] - kg[0]) / (4 * sig**2) return np.array([np.sum(wgt * omk), np.sum(wgt * kg)]) eta = 0.5 L = np.array([[np.cosh(eta), np.sinh(eta)], [np.sinh(eta), np.cosh(eta)]]) Li = np.linalg.inv(L) P0 = dP(hfun, 1.25, 2.4, 500) P1 = dP(lambda t, x: hfun(Li[0, 0] * t + Li[0, 1] * x, Li[1, 0] * t + Li[1, 1] * x), 3.2, 3.6, 900) print(' (d) Delta P(h) = (%.8f, %.8f), squared invariant mass %.8f' % (P0[0], P0[1], P0[0]**2 - P0[1]**2)) print(' boost of rapidity 0.5: |Delta P(Lambda h) - Lambda Delta P(h)| = %.1e' % np.abs(P1 - L @ P0).max()) print('C3. Poisson-located Gaussian collapses, massless field in 1+1 dimensions') # Collapse regions are causal diamonds of half-size dl in light-cone coordinates # u = t - x, v = t + x, with product smearings beta(u) beta(v). zn, zw = np.polynomial.legendre.leggauss(200) Ib = np.sum(zw * bump(zn)) beta = lambda z: bump(z) / Ib # normalized bump on [-1, 1] gn, gw = np.polynomial.legendre.leggauss(64) def Bcdf(z): z = np.clip(z, -1, 1) s = -1 + np.outer(z + 1, gn + 1) / 2 return (z + 1) / 2 * (beta(s) @ gw) def Sfun(dd): """int int beta(z) beta(z') sgn(z - z' + dd); exactly +-1 for disjoint supports""" if dd >= 2: return 1.0 if dd <= -2: return -1.0 if dd < 0: return -Sfun(-dd) return np.sum(zw * beta(zn) * (2 * Bcdf(zn + dd) - 1)) def relation(pts, dl): """R[i, j]: region j meets the causal future of region i""" u, v = pts[:, 0] - pts[:, 1], pts[:, 0] + pts[:, 1] R = (u[None, :] - u[:, None] > -2 * dl) & (v[None, :] - v[:, None] > -2 * dl) np.fill_diagonal(R, False) return R def geometry(pts, dl, scale): """relation and E_ij = E(h_i, h_j), with E(t, x) = -(sgn u + sgn v)/4""" n = len(pts) u, v = pts[:, 0] - pts[:, 1], pts[:, 0] + pts[:, 1] E = np.zeros((n, n)) for i in range(n): for j in range(i + 1, n): val = -scale**2 / 16 * (Sfun((u[i] - u[j]) / dl) + Sfun((v[i] - v[j]) / dl)) E[i, j], E[j, i] = val, -val return relation(pts, dl), E def topo(R, rng=None): """a linear extension of R (random if rng is given); None if R has a cycle""" n = len(R) indeg = R.sum(0).astype(int) order, avail = [], [i for i in range(n) if indeg[i] == 0] while avail: k = avail.pop(rng.integers(len(avail)) if rng is not None else 0) order.append(k) for j in np.nonzero(R[k])[0]: indeg[j] -= 1 if indeg[j] == 0: avail.append(j) return order if len(order) == n else None def cov_closed(order, E, Sig, sg): """outcome covariance along an order: Sigma + diag(sigma^2) + kicks of earlier collapses""" C = Sig + np.diag(sg**2) for pos, m in enumerate(order): e = np.zeros(len(order)) for m2 in order[pos + 1:]: e[m2] = E[m2, m] C = C + np.outer(e, e) / (4 * sg[m]**2) return C def absE(E): w_, V_ = eigh(1j * E) return (V_ @ np.diag(np.abs(w_)) @ V_.conj().T).real rng = np.random.default_rng(2027) dl, scale = 0.5, 4.0 cnt = rng.poisson(6.0 * 16.0, size=20000) print(' (a) counts, area 16, intensity 6: mean %.2f, variance %.2f (expected 96 and 96)' % (cnt.mean(), cnt.var())) lam_b = 1.5 print(' (b) intensity 1.5, 40000 samples per box: side P(orderable) bound of Prop. 13.22') for side in (1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 2.5): ok = 0 for _ in range(40000): pts = rng.uniform(0, side, size=(rng.poisson(lam_b * side * side), 2)) ok += topo(relation(pts, dl)) is not None lv, Ncell = lam_b * dl**2, int(round(side / dl))**2 print(' %.1f %.5f %.5f' % (side, ok / 40000, (np.exp(-lv) * (1 + lv))**Ncell)) lam_c, side = 0.4, 4.0 nconf, worst, nev, next_, t_ext = 0, 0.0, 0, 0, 0 while nconf < 300: pts = rng.uniform(0, side, size=(rng.poisson(lam_c * side * side), 2)) if len(pts) < 2 or topo(relation(pts, dl)) is None: continue n = len(pts) R, E = geometry(pts, dl, scale) sg = rng.uniform(0.5, 1.2, size=n) Sig = 0.5 * absE(E) + 0.3 * np.eye(n) orders = {tuple(topo(R, rng)) for _ in range(40)} Cs = [cov_closed(list(o), E, Sig, sg) for o in orders] worst = max(worst, max(np.abs(C - Cs[0]).max() for C in Cs)) nconf, nev, next_ = nconf + 1, nev + n, next_ + len(orders) t_ext += all(pts[i, 0] < pts[k, 0] for i, k in zip(*np.nonzero(R))) print(' (c) %d orderable configurations, %d collapses, %d linear extensions:' % (nconf, nev, next_)) print(' max covariance difference between linear extensions %.1e' % worst) def realize(E): """one-mode realization phi_i = cu_i q + cw_i p of a rank-2 antisymmetric E""" T, Z = schur(E, output='real') k = next(k for k in range(len(E) - 1) if abs(T[k, k + 1]) > 1e-12) b, uu, ww = T[k, k + 1], Z[:, k], Z[:, k + 1] if b < 0: b, uu, ww = -b, ww, uu cu, cw = np.sqrt(b) * uu, np.sqrt(b) * ww assert np.abs(np.outer(cu, cw) - np.outer(cw, cu) - E).max() < 1e-12 return cu, cw ncut = 140 a = ladder(ncut) qo, po = (a + a.T) / np.sqrt(2), 1j * (a.T - a) / np.sqrt(2) edges = [-np.inf, -0.7, 0.0, 0.7, np.inf] def run_order(order, eigs, mult): """apply the maps rho -> V (M o V* rho V) V* in the given order to the vacuum; trace""" rho = np.zeros((ncut, ncut), complex) rho[0, 0] = 1 for m in order: w_, V_ = eigs[m] rho = V_ @ ((V_.conj().T @ rho @ V_) * mult(m, w_)) @ V_.conj().T return np.trace(rho) def bin_mult(bins, sg): def mb(m, w_): dd, mm = w_[:, None] - w_[None, :], (w_[:, None] + w_[None, :]) / 2 lo, hi = edges[bins[m]], edges[bins[m] + 1] mass_in_bin = Phi((hi - mm) / sg[m]) - Phi((lo - mm) / sg[m]) return np.exp(-dd**2 / (8 * sg[m]**2)) * mass_in_bin return mb def bin_probability(rho, eig, b, sgy): """probability that a Gaussian collapse of width sgy lands in bin b (eig: its observable)""" w_, V_ = eig dd, mm = w_[:, None] - w_[None, :], (w_[:, None] + w_[None, :]) / 2 in_bin = Phi((edges[b + 1] - mm) / sgy) - Phi((edges[b] - mm) / sgy) M = np.exp(-dd**2 / (8 * sgy**2)) * in_bin return np.trace((V_.conj().T @ rho @ V_) * M).real def binned_law(order, eigs, sg, k): law = np.zeros((4,) * k) for bins in np.ndindex(*law.shape): law[bins] = run_order(order, eigs, bin_mult(bins, sg)).real return law found, cf_dev, ord_dev, norm_dev = 0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0 while found < 4: pts = rng.uniform(0, 1.6, size=(3, 2)) if topo(relation(pts, dl)) is None: continue R, E = geometry(pts, dl, scale) orders = list({tuple(topo(R, rng)) for _ in range(30)}) if np.sum(np.abs(E) > 1e-3) < 4 or len(orders) < 2: continue found += 1 cu, cw = realize(E) eigs = [eigh(cu[i] * qo + cw[i] * po) for i in range(3)] Sig = 0.5 * np.outer(cu, cu) + 0.5 * np.outer(cw, cw) sg = np.array([0.8, 0.9, 1.0]) for s in rng.normal(size=(4, 3)): def ms(m, w_, s=s): dd, mm = w_[:, None] - w_[None, :], (w_[:, None] + w_[None, :]) / 2 return np.exp(-dd**2 / (8 * sg[m]**2) + 1j * s[m] * mm - s[m]**2 * sg[m]**2 / 2) for o in orders: closed = np.exp(-0.5 * s @ cov_closed(list(o), E, Sig, sg) @ s) cf_dev = max(cf_dev, abs(run_order(o, eigs, ms) - closed)) laws = [binned_law(o, eigs, sg, 3) for o in orders] norm_dev = max(norm_dev, max(abs(l.sum() - 1) for l in laws)) ord_dev = max(ord_dev, max(np.abs(l - laws[0]).max() for l in laws)) print(' (d) 4 three-collapse configurations, one-mode realization, Fock cutoff %d:' % ncut) print(' characteristic function, direct vs closed form: max dev %.1e' % cf_dev) print(' 64-cell binned history laws: max difference between linear extensions %.1e,' % ord_dev) print(' normalization error %.1e' % norm_dev) pts = np.array([[0.3, 0.1], [0.55, 0.25]]) # two overlapping diamonds R, E = geometry(pts, dl, scale) cu, cw = realize(E) eigs = [eigh(cu[i] * qo + cw[i] * po) for i in range(2)] sg = np.array([0.8, 0.9]) rho = np.zeros((ncut, ncut), complex) rho[0, 0] = 1 A2 = cu[1] * qo + cw[1] * po rho1 = dephase(rho, cu[0] * qo + cw[0] * po, sg[0]) var_after = (np.trace(rho1 @ A2 @ A2) - np.trace(rho1 @ A2)**2).real var_alone = (np.trace(rho @ A2 @ A2) - np.trace(rho @ A2)**2).real print(' (e) overlapping pair, E(h1, h2) = %.6f:' % E[0, 1]) print(' increase of Var(y2) caused by collapse 1: %.10f; E^2/(4 sigma1^2) = %.10f' % (var_after - var_alone, E[0, 1]**2 / (4 * sg[0]**2))) l12, l21 = binned_law((0, 1), eigs, sg, 2), binned_law((1, 0), eigs, sg, 2) print(' binned joint laws for the two orders: total-variation distance %.4f' % (0.5 * np.abs(l12 - l21).sum())) A1 = cu[0] * qo + cw[0] * po r12 = dephase(dephase(rho, A1, sg[0]), A2, sg[1]) r21 = dephase(dephase(rho, A2, sg[1]), A1, sg[0]) print(' unread channels in the two orders: max difference of the final states %.1e' % np.abs(r12 - r21).max()) eta, nbad, nchg, maxd = 0.6, 0, 0, 0.0 while nbad < 300: pts = rng.uniform(0, 2.0, size=(rng.poisson(6.0), 2)) if len(pts) < 2 or topo(relation(pts, dl)) is not None: continue n = len(pts) R, E = geometry(pts, dl, scale) sg = np.full(n, 0.8) Sig = 0.5 * absE(E) + 0.3 * np.eye(n) o1 = list(np.argsort(pts[:, 0])) o2 = list(np.argsort(np.cosh(eta) * pts[:, 0] - np.sinh(eta) * pts[:, 1])) dC = np.abs(cov_closed(o1, E, Sig, sg) - cov_closed(o2, E, Sig, sg)).max() nbad, nchg, maxd = nbad + 1, nchg + (dC > 1e-12), max(maxd, dC) print(' (f) %d non-orderable configurations ordered by centre time in two frames' % nbad) print(' (relative rapidity 0.6): laws differ in %d, max covariance difference %.3f' % (nchg, maxd)) print(' (g) centre-time order is a linear extension of the causal order in %d of the %d' % (t_ext, nconf)) print(' orderable configurations of item (c)') # (h) cosmological-time-ordered collapses on a non-orderable Sorkin-type configuration: # j (tested operation), c1 and c2 (overlapping law collapses), y (target), applied in order # of t. Light-cone centres (u, v) give j < c1 < y causally, j and y causally separated. uvc = np.array([[0.0, 0.0], [-0.525, 1.2], [-0.225, 1.05], [-1.05, 2.3]]) pts = np.column_stack([(uvc[:, 0] + uvc[:, 1]) / 2, (uvc[:, 1] - uvc[:, 0]) / 2]) R, E = geometry(pts, dl, 8.0) print(' (h) order j, c1, c2, y by t; c1 and c2 overlap; E(h_j, h_c1) = %.4f, E(h_c1, h_y) = %.4f,' % (E[0, 1], E[1, 3])) print(' E(h_c1, h_c2) = %.4f, E(h_j, h_y) = %.1f' % (E[1, 2], abs(E[0, 3]))) gl = lambda x: (1 + np.tanh(2 * x)) / 2 for nc in (36, 44): a1, a2 = modes(nc, 2) q1, q2 = [(a + a.T) / np.sqrt(2) for a in (a1, a2)] p1, p2 = [1j * (a.T - a) / np.sqrt(2) for a in (a1, a2)] # two-mode realization of the commutation data with phi_j on mode 1 and phi_y on mode 2 phis = [q1, E[1, 2] / E[0, 2] * q1 + E[0, 1] * p1 - E[1, 3] * p2, E[0, 2] * p1 - E[2, 3] * p2, q2] for i in range(4): for k in range(4): c = (phis[i] @ phis[k] - phis[k] @ phis[i])[:8, :8] assert np.abs(c - 1j * E[i, k] * np.eye(8)).max() < 1e-9 ey = eigh(phis[3]) w1, V1 = eigh(phis[1]) w0, V0 = eigh(phis[0]) def target_law(at_j, luders): rho = np.zeros((nc * nc, nc * nc), complex) rho[0, 0] = 1 if at_j == 'kick': U = expm(3j * phis[0]) rho = U @ rho @ U.conj().T elif at_j == 'collapse': rho = dephase(rho, phis[0], 0.8) elif at_j == 'luders': # an operation outside the Weyl-diagonal class K = [V0 @ np.diag(np.sqrt(gl(w0))) @ V0.conj().T, V0 @ np.diag(np.sqrt(1 - gl(w0))) @ V0.conj().T] rho = sum(k @ rho @ k.conj().T for k in K) if luders: K = [V1 @ np.diag(np.sqrt(gl(w1))) @ V1.conj().T, V1 @ np.diag(np.sqrt(1 - gl(w1))) @ V1.conj().T] rho = sum(k @ rho @ k.conj().T for k in K) else: rho = dephase(rho, phis[1], 0.8) rho = dephase(rho, phis[2], 0.9) return np.array([bin_probability(rho, ey, b, 1.0) for b in range(4)]) base = target_law(None, False) dk = np.abs(target_law('kick', False) - base).max() dc = np.abs(target_law('collapse', False) - base).max() lb = target_law(None, True) dlk = np.abs(target_law('kick', True) - lb).max() print(' cutoff %d: Gaussian c1: change of the target law by a kick at j %.1e,' % (nc, dk)) dlj = np.abs(target_law('luders', False) - base).max() print(' by a collapse at j %.1e; target law sums to %.12f' % (dc, base.sum())) print(' unsharp Lueders instrument at j (not Weyl-diagonal): change %.1e' % dlj) print(' unsharp Lueders c1 (negative control): change by the kick, largest bin %.7f' % dlk) print('C4. Gapless pointer realization of the collapses (Proposition 13.31)') # Each collapse is realized by a pointer (q_k, p_k) with no Hamiltonian, coupled through the # density p_k h_k(x) phi(x). The matrix M below is Gamma_kl = E_ret(h_k, h_l), the retarded # part of E, with E_ret(t, x) = -theta(u) theta(v)/2, so that E = Gamma - Gamma^T. def Mret(pts, dl, scale): u, v = pts[:, 0] - pts[:, 1], pts[:, 0] + pts[:, 1] n = len(pts) M = np.zeros((n, n)) for k in range(n): for l in range(n): M[k, l] = -scale**2 / 32 * ((1 + Sfun((u[k] - u[l]) / dl)) * (1 + Sfun((v[k] - v[l]) / dl))) return M def ordered_sums(pts, dl, scale, nn, eta): """sum of E(x_a - x_b) w_a w_b over node pairs with x_a later than x_b in the time cosh(eta) t - sinh(eta) x: the phase of the time-ordered product of the couplings""" z, wz = np.polynomial.legendre.leggauss(nn) Zu, Zv = np.meshgrid(z, z, indexing='ij') w = (scale / 2) * np.outer(beta(z) * wz, beta(z) * wz).ravel() nodes = [((t - x) + dl * Zu.ravel(), (t + x) + dl * Zv.ravel()) for t, x in pts] T = np.zeros((len(pts), len(pts))) for k in range(len(pts)): for l in range(len(pts)): for c in range(0, len(w), 512): du = nodes[k][0][c:c + 512, None] - nodes[l][0][None, :] dv = nodes[k][1][c:c + 512, None] - nodes[l][1][None, :] later = np.cosh(eta) * (du + dv) - np.sinh(eta) * (dv - du) > 0 Ev = np.where(later, -(np.sign(du) + np.sign(dv)) / 4, 0.0) T[k, l] += w[c:c + 512] @ Ev @ w return T def coherent_states(alphas, nc): C = np.zeros((len(alphas), nc), complex) C[:, 0] = np.exp(-np.abs(alphas)**2 / 2) for n in range(1, nc): C[:, n] = C[:, n - 1] * alphas / np.sqrt(n) return C dp, npg = 0.12, 81 pg = (np.arange(npg) - npg // 2) * dp def pointer_states(cu, cw, M, sg, chirp): """field vector for each pair of pointer momenta after the coupling U, pointers prepared in Gaussians of widths sg, pre-chirped by exp(i M_kk p_k^2/2) if chirp""" P = np.stack([a.ravel() for a in np.meshgrid(pg, pg, indexing='ij')], 1) amp = np.prod((2 * sg**2 / np.pi)**0.25 * np.exp(-sg**2 * P**2), axis=1) ph = -0.5 * np.einsum('gk,kl,gl->g', P, M, P) + (0.5 * P**2 @ np.diag(M) if chirp else 0) alph = (P @ cw - 1j * (P @ cu)) / np.sqrt(2) # exp(-i p.phi)|0> is coherent return (amp * np.exp(1j * ph))[:, None] * coherent_states(alph, ncut) def chi_pointer(F, n1, n2): """E exp(i s.y) = sum_p <F(p), F(p - s)> for s = (n1, n2) grid steps""" G = F.reshape(npg, npg, -1) A = G[max(n1, 0):npg + min(n1, 0), max(n2, 0):npg + min(n2, 0)] B = G[max(-n1, 0):npg + min(-n1, 0), max(-n2, 0):npg + min(-n2, 0)] return np.sum(A.conj() * B) * dp * dp def chi_sequential(order, eigs, sg, s): def ms(m, w_): dd, mm = w_[:, None] - w_[None, :], (w_[:, None] + w_[None, :]) / 2 return np.exp(-dd**2 / (8 * sg[m]**2) + 1j * s[m] * mm - s[m]**2 * sg[m]**2 / 2) return run_order(order, eigs, ms) shifts = [(1, 0), (0, 1), (2, -1), (3, 2), (-2, 4), (5, 3), (-4, -3)] sg = np.array([0.8, 0.9]) res = {} for name, pts in (('overlap', np.array([[0.3, 0.1], [0.55, 0.25]])), ('one-way', np.array([[0.0, 0.0], [1.2, 0.3]]))): R, E = geometry(pts, dl, scale) M = Mret(pts, dl, scale) r = {'E': E, 'M': M, 'R': R, 'split': np.abs(M - M.T - E).max()} T0 = {nn: ordered_sums(pts, dl, scale, nn, 0.0) for nn in (32, 64)} T1 = ordered_sums(pts, dl, scale, 64, 0.6) r['fol'] = np.abs(T1 - T0[64]).max() r['disc'] = [np.abs(T0[nn] - M).max() for nn in (32, 64)] cu, cw = realize(E) Sig = 0.5 * np.outer(cu, cu) + 0.5 * np.outer(cw, cw) D = np.diag(1 / (4 * sg**2)) Mo = M - np.diag(np.diag(M)) r['C'] = Sig + np.diag(sg**2) + Mo @ D @ Mo.T r['Cfull'] = Sig + np.diag(sg**2) + M @ D @ M.T r['Cord'] = cov_closed([0, 1], E, Sig, sg) eigs = [eigh(cu[i] * qo + cw[i] * po) for i in range(2)] dev = {} for chirp in (True, False): F = pointer_states(cu, cw, M, sg, chirp) C = r['C'] if chirp else r['Cfull'] dev[chirp] = max(abs(chi_pointer(F, *n) - np.exp(-0.5 * dp**2 * np.array(n) @ C @ np.array(n))) for n in shifts) if chirp: r['seq'] = max(abs(chi_pointer(F, *n) - chi_sequential([0, 1], eigs, sg, dp * np.array(n))) for n in shifts) rho_p = (F.T @ F.conj()) * dp * dp rho0 = np.zeros((ncut, ncut), complex) rho0[0, 0] = 1 rho_s = dephase(dephase(rho0, cu[0] * qo + cw[0] * po, sg[0]), cu[1] * qo + cw[1] * po, sg[1]) r['unread'] = np.abs(rho_p - rho_s)[:60, :60].max() r['trace'] = np.trace(rho_p).real r['dev'] = dev res[name] = r ov, ow = res['overlap'], res['one-way'] print(' (a) E = Gamma - Gamma^T: max dev %.1e (overlapping pair), %.1e (one-way pair)' % (ov['split'], ow['split'])) print(' overlapping pair: Gamma_12 = %.6f, Gamma_21 = %.6f, Gamma_11 = Gamma_22 = %.6f' % (ov['M'][0, 1], ov['M'][1, 0], ov['M'][0, 0])) print(' phase of the time-ordered product, two foliations (relative rapidity 0.6):') print(' max difference %.1e and %.1e;' % (ov['fol'], ow['fol'])) print(' deviation from Gamma, 32 and 64 nodes per axis: %.1e, %.1e (overlapping),' % tuple(ov['disc'])) print(' %.1e, %.1e (one-way)' % tuple(ow['disc'])) print(' (b) overlapping pair, explicit pointers, characteristic function vs closed form:') print(' pre-chirped pointers %.1e; without the pre-chirp, full Gamma, %.1e' % (ov['dev'][True], ov['dev'][False])) print(' (c) unread field channel vs two Gaussian collapse channels: max dev %.1e;' % ov['unread']) print(' trace %.12f' % ov['trace']) print(' (d) one-way pair: pointers vs Gaussian collapses applied in causal order, direct') print(' characteristic functions: max dev %.1e; closed-form covariances: max diff %.1e' % (ow['seq'], np.abs(ow['C'] - ow['Cord']).max())) dC = ov['C'] - ov['Cord'] print(' overlapping pair, pointer law minus the law of collapses in centre-time order:') print(' Var(y1) %+.6f, Var(y2) %+.6f, Cov(y1, y2) %+.6f;' % (dC[0, 0], dC[1, 1], dC[0, 1])) print(' direct characteristic functions differ by up to %.4f' % ov['seq']) Output: C1. Physical states are Psi(t_A, t_B) = U_Delta(t_A) chi(Delta), Delta = t_A - t_B, # where U_Delta solves i dU/ds = H_Delta(s) U from s0 = -2.5, with # H_Delta(s) = H_S + sum_{k keyed to A} g_k(s) V_k + sum_{k keyed to B} g_k(s - Delta) V_k. import numpy as np from scipy.linalg import expm sx = np.array([[0, 1], [1, 0]], complex) sy = np.array([[0, -1j], [1j, 0]]) sz = np.diag([1.0, -1.0]).astype(complex) P1 = np.diag([0.0, 1.0]).astype(complex) I2 = np.eye(2) kron = np.kron bump = lambda z: np.where(np.abs(z) < 1, np.exp(-1 / np.maximum(1 - z**2, 1e-300)), 0.0) ds, s0, s1 = 0.01, -2.5, 7.0 sgrid = s0 + ds * np.arange(int(round((s1 - s0) / ds)) + 1) mids = sgrid[:-1] + ds / 2 def pulse(area): """switching function supported in [0, 1]; its midpoint sum on the grid equals area""" g = lambda s: bump((s - 0.5) / 0.5) norm = np.sum(g(np.arange(0, 1, ds) + ds / 2)) * ds return lambda s: area * g(s) / norm def propagate(Hfun): """U(s) from s0 at every grid point""" U = np.eye(4, dtype=complex) out = [U] for m in mids: U = expm(-1j * ds * Hfun(m)) @ U out.append(U) return out at = lambda s: int(round((s - s0) / ds)) rng = np.random.default_rng(5) def system_state(): v = rng.normal(size=2) + 1j * rng.normal(size=2) return np.kron(v / np.linalg.norm(v), [1, 0]) # record starts in |0> def packets(centres, width, states): """chi(Delta) on a grid of offsets: wave packets in Delta carrying the given states""" Ds = np.round(np.arange(-3.0, 3.0 + ds / 2, ds), 10) chi = np.zeros((len(Ds), 4), complex) for c, st in zip(centres, states): chi += bump((Ds - c) / width)[:, None] * st[None, :] chi /= np.sqrt(np.sum(np.abs(chi)**2) * ds) keep = np.abs(chi).sum(1) > 0 return Ds[keep], chi[keep] print('Case 1: one record written by an interaction keyed to clock A') HS = kron(0.35 * sz, I2) V1 = kron(P1, sx) + 0.6 * kron(sx, I2) g1 = pulse(np.pi / 2) Us = propagate(lambda s: HS + g1(s) * V1) # independent of Delta here E = kron(I2, P1) # the record reads 1 Ds, chi = packets([0.3, 1.4], 0.25, [system_state(), system_state()]) sstar = 1.0 O = lambda c: Us[at(sstar)].conj().T @ E @ Us[at(sstar)] @ c P_phys = sum(np.vdot(O(c), O(c)).real for c in chi) * ds def P_A(tau): # integral over t_B = tau - Delta of |E Psi(tau, t_B)|^2 return sum(np.linalg.norm(E @ Us[at(tau)] @ c)**2 for c in chi) * ds def P_B(taup): # integral over t_A = taup + Delta of |E Psi(t_A, taup)|^2 return sum(np.linalg.norm(E @ Us[at(taup + D)] @ c)**2 for D, c in zip(Ds, chi)) * ds print(' offsets Delta in [%.2f, %.2f]; interaction in A-time [0, 1]' % (Ds.min(), Ds.max())) print(' P_phys = %.12f P_A(2.0) = %.12f P_A(4.0) = %.12f' % (P_phys, P_A(2.0), P_A(4.0))) print(' P_B(%.2f) = %.12f P_B(3.0) = %.12f' % (1 - Ds.min(), P_B(1 - Ds.min()), P_B(3.0))) print(' A-time: ' + ', '.join('P_A(%.1f) = %.6f' % (t, P_A(t)) for t in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0))) print(' B-time: ' + ', '.join('P_B(%.1f) = %.6f' % (t, P_B(t)) for t in (-1.0, -0.4, 0.2, 0.6))) print(' O is a projector on the sector: max |O^2 - O| = %.1e' % max(np.abs(O(O(c)) - O(c)).max() for c in chi)) print('Case 2: interaction 1 (keyed to A) rotates S; interaction 2 (keyed to B) records S') V1, V2 = kron(sy, I2), kron(P1, sx) g1, g2 = pulse(np.pi / 8), pulse(np.pi / 2) psi_neg, psi_pos = system_state(), system_state() Ds, chi = packets([-1.6, 1.6], 0.25, [psi_neg, psi_pos]) UD = [propagate(lambda s, D=D: g1(s) * V1 + g2(s - D) * V2) for D in Ds] sstar = 1.0 + Ds.max() P_phys = sum(np.linalg.norm(E @ U[at(sstar)] @ c)**2 for U, c in zip(UD, chi)) * ds PA = sum(np.linalg.norm(E @ U[at(4.0)] @ c)**2 for U, c in zip(UD, chi)) * ds taup = sstar - Ds.min() PB = sum(np.linalg.norm(E @ U[at(taup + D)] @ c)**2 for U, D, c in zip(UD, Ds, chi)) * ds G1, G2 = expm(-1j * np.pi / 8 * V1), expm(-1j * np.pi / 2 * V2) mu = [np.sum(np.abs(chi[Ds < 0])**2) * ds, np.sum(np.abs(chi[Ds > 0])**2) * ds] p_rec_first = np.linalg.norm(E @ G1 @ G2 @ psi_neg)**2 p_rot_first = np.linalg.norm(E @ G2 @ G1 @ psi_pos)**2 print(' P_phys = %.12f P_A(4.0) = %.12f P_B(%.2f) = %.12f' % (P_phys, PA, taup, PB)) print(' definite orders: record first %.6f (weight %.4f), rotation first %.6f (weight %.4f)' % (p_rec_first, mu[0], p_rot_first, mu[1])) print(' weighted mixture of the two definite orders = %.12f' % (mu[0] * p_rec_first + mu[1] * p_rot_first)) print('Case 3: the same interactions, an offset-coherent observable (H_S = 0)') psi0 = system_state() Ds, chi = packets([-1.6, 1.6], 0.25, [psi0, psi0]) UD = [propagate(lambda s, D=D: g1(s) * V1 + g2(s - D) * V2) for D in Ds] fplus = bump((Ds + 1.6) / 0.25) + bump((Ds - 1.6) / 0.25) fplus = fplus / np.sqrt(np.sum(fplus**2) * ds) # coherent superposition of offsets amp_phys = sum(fp * (U[at(sstar)] @ c) for fp, U, c in zip(fplus, UD, chi)) * ds amp_A = sum(fp * (U[at(4.0)] @ c) for fp, U, c in zip(fplus, UD, chi)) * ds amp_B = sum(fp * (U[at(taup + D)] @ c) for fp, U, D, c in zip(fplus, UD, Ds, chi)) * ds W1, W2 = G1 @ G2, G2 @ G1 # offset -1.6: record first; +1.6: rotation first pred = 0.5 * (1 + np.vdot(W1 @ psi0, W2 @ psi0).real) mix = 0.0 for sgn in (-1, 1): # incoherent mixture of the two offsets sel = np.sign(Ds) == sgn ch = chi * sel[:, None] / np.sqrt(np.sum(np.abs(chi[sel])**2) * ds) a_ = sum(fp * (U[at(sstar)] @ c) for fp, U, c in zip(fplus, UD, ch)) * ds mix += 0.5 * np.vdot(a_, a_).real print(' P(+): clock-neutral %.12f, relative to A %.12f, relative to B %.12f' % (np.vdot(amp_phys, amp_phys).real, np.vdot(amp_A, amp_A).real, np.vdot(amp_B, amp_B).real)) print(' (1 + Re<W1 psi, W2 psi>)/2 = %.12f; incoherent mixture of offsets gives %.12f' % (pred, mix)) Output: Case 1: one record written by an interaction keyed to clock A offsets Delta in [0.06, 1.64]; interaction in A-time [0, 1] P_phys = 0.139627690767 P_A(2.0) = 0.139627690767 P_A(4.0) = 0.139627690767 P_B(0.94) = 0.139627690767 P_B(3.0) = 0.139627690767 A-time: P_A(-0.5) = 0.000000, P_A(0.0) = 0.000000, P_A(0.5) = 0.153583, P_A(1.0) = 0.139628 B-time: P_B(-1.0) = 0.021295, P_B(-0.4) = 0.032189, P_B(0.2) = 0.145553, P_B(0.6) = 0.144791 O is a projector on the sector: max |O^2 - O| = 4.0e-15 Case 2: interaction 1 (keyed to A) rotates S; interaction 2 (keyed to B) records S P_phys = 0.706791827009 P_A(4.0) = 0.706791827009 P_B(4.68) = 0.706791827009 definite orders: record first 0.868488 (weight 0.5000), rotation first 0.545095 (weight 0.5000) weighted mixture of the two definite orders = 0.706791827009 Case 3: the same interactions, an offset-coherent observable (H_S = 0) P(+): clock-neutral 0.926776695297, relative to A 0.926776695297, relative to B 0.926776695297 (1 + Re<W1 psi, W2 psi>)/2 = 0.926776695297; incoherent mixture of offsets gives 0.500000000000
